Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a time_series algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 197. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 394/4.
